#[soroban_sdk::contractargs(name = "Args")]
#[soroban_sdk::contractclient(name = "Client")]
pub trait Contract {
    fn get_po(env: soroban_sdk::Env, po_id: u64) -> Result<PurchaseOrder, PoError>;
    fn fund_po(env: soroban_sdk::Env, po_id: u64) -> Result<(), PoError>;
    fn get_line(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        line_id: u32,
    ) -> Result<PoLine, PoError>;
    fn accept_po(env: soroban_sdk::Env, po_id: u64) -> Result<(), PoError>;
    fn cancel_po(env: soroban_sdk::Env, po_id: u64) -> Result<(), PoError>;
    fn create_po(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        buyer: soroban_sdk::Address,
        supplier: soroban_sdk::Address,
        token: soroban_sdk::Address,
        lines: soroban_sdk::Vec<LineInput>,
    ) -> Result<(), PoError>;
    fn get_receipt(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        receipt_id: u64,
    ) -> Result<Receipt, PoError>;
    fn receipt_value(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        receipt_id: u64,
    ) -> Result<i128, PoError>;
    fn reject_receipt(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        receipt_id: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), PoError>;
    fn submit_receipt(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        receipt_id: u64,
        quantities: soroban_sdk::Vec<ReceiptQuantityInput>,
    ) -> Result<(), PoError>;
    fn get_receipt_line(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        receipt_id: u64,
        line_id: u32,
    ) -> Result<ReceiptLine, PoError>;
    fn accept_receipt_and_settle(
        env: soroban_sdk::Env,
        po_id: u64,
        receipt_id: u64,
    ) -> Result<i128, PoError>;
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub struct PoLine {
    pub accepted_quantity: u64,
    pub line_id: u32,
    pub ordered_quantity: u64,
    pub unit_price: i128,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub struct Receipt {
    pub line_count: u32,
    pub line_ids: soroban_sdk::Vec<u32>,
    pub po_id: u64,
    pub receipt_id: u64,
    pub status: ReceiptStatus,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub struct LineInput {
    pub line_id: u32,
    pub ordered_quantity: u64,
    pub unit_price: i128,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub struct ReceiptLine {
    pub line_id: u32,
    pub quantity_delta: u64,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub struct PurchaseOrder {
    pub buyer: soroban_sdk::Address,
    pub funded_value: i128,
    pub line_count: u32,
    pub line_ids: soroban_sdk::Vec<u32>,
    pub paid_value: i128,
    pub po_id: u64,
    pub refunded_value: i128,
    pub status: PoStatus,
    pub supplier: soroban_sdk::Address,
    pub token: soroban_sdk::Address,
    pub total_value: i128,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub struct ReceiptQuantityInput {
    pub line_id: u32,
    pub quantity_delta: u64,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub enum PoStatus {
    Draft,
    Accepted,
    Funded,
    Closed,
    Cancelled,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracttype]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub enum ReceiptStatus {
    Submitted,
    Rejected,
    Settled,
}
#[soroban_sdk::contracterror]
#[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone, Eq, PartialEq, Ord, PartialOrd)]
pub enum PoError {
    AlreadyExists = 1,
    NotFound = 2,
    InvalidPoId = 3,
    InvalidLine = 4,
    DuplicateLineId = 5,
    TooManyLines = 6,
    ArithmeticOverflow = 7,
    InvalidParties = 8,
    InvalidState = 9,
    ReceiptAlreadyExists = 10,
    InvalidReceipt = 11,
    DuplicateReceiptLine = 12,
    UnknownPoLine = 13,
    QuantityExceedsRemaining = 14,
    InvalidReceiptState = 15,
    ReceiptNotFound = 16,
    SettlementExceedsFunded = 17,
}
